264→        self.side_tongue = self._trial_velocity(SIDE_TONGUE)\n   265→        self.bottom_tongue = self._trial_velocity(BOTTOM_TONGUE)\n   266→        self.paw = self._trial_velocity(PAW)\n   267→\n   268→        # the two cameras see the tongue at different pixel scales\n   269→        self.side_scale = self._view_scale(self.side_tongue)\n   270→        self.bottom_scale = self._view_scale(self.bottom_tongue)\n   271→\n   272→    def _trial_velocity(self, feature):\n   273→        \"\"\"Frame resolution velocity of one tracked feature, one entry per trial.\"\"\"\n   274→        return {trial: self._velocity(self._track(trial, feature)) for trial in self.trials}\n   275→\n   276→    def _video_offset(self):\n   277→        \"\"\"Seconds by which the video clock leads the behavior clock (findVideoOffset.m).\"\"\"\n   278→        sample_rate = float(np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['fs'])[0])\n   279→        video_start = np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['bitcode']['bitstart']) / sample_rate\n   280→        behavior_start = trial_column(self.obj['bp'], 'ev', 'bitStart')\n   281→        return pd.Series(video_start).mode().iloc[0] - pd.Series(behavior_start).mode().iloc[0]\n   282→\n   283→    def _traj(self, trial, view):\n   284→        \"\"\"Tracking of one trial and camera, however the file stores it.\"\"\"\n   285→        traj = self.obj['traj'][view]\n   286→        if isinstance(traj, dict):                     # v7.3: one entry per camera\n   287→            return {field: traj[field][trial] for field in traj}\n   288→        entry = traj[trial]                            # v5: one entry per trial\n   289→        if hasattr(entry, '_fieldnames'):              # scipy leaves nested structs as objects\n   290→            return {field: getattr(entry, field) for field in entry._fieldnames}\n   291→        return entry\n   292→\n   293→    # camera frame time after correcting for the video offset and aligning to the go cue, in seconds\n   294→    def _frame_time(self, trial, view):\n   295→        \"\"\"Frame times of one trial and camera, in seconds from go cue onset.\"\"\"\n   296→        frame_times = np.ravel(self._traj(trial, view)['frameTimes'])\n   297→        return frame_times - self.offset - self.go_cue[trial]\n   298→\n   299→    def _camera_of(self, trial, feature):\n   300→        \"\"\"The camera that tracks a feature, and its tracking for this trial.\"\"\"\n   301→        for view in (SIDE, BOTTOM):\n   302→            traj = self._traj(trial, view)\n   303→            if feature in list(traj['featNames']):     # the name picks out the camera\n   304→                return view, traj\n   305→        raise KeyError('%s is tracked by neither camera' % feature)\n   306→\n   307→    # x, y, and likelihood within the time window\n   308→    def _track(self, trial, feature):\n   309→        \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature over the frames inside the window.\"\"\"\n   310→        view, traj = self._camera_of(trial, feature)\n   311→        x, y, likelihood = _feature(traj, list(traj['featNames']).index(feature))\n   312→        time = self._frame_time(trial, view)           # frame counts can differ between views\n   313→        inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n   314→        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n   315→\n   316→    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n   317→    def _velocity(self, track):\n   318→        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n   319→        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n   320→        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n   321→        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n   322→            return time, speed\n   323→\n   324→        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n   325→        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n   326→\n   327→        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n   328→        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n   329→            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n   330→                continue\n   331→            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n   332→            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n   333→            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n   334→                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n   335→        return time, speed\n   336→\n   337→    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n   338→        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n   339→        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n   340→        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n   341→\n   342→    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n   343→    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n   344→    def tongue_velocity(self):\n   345→        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   346→        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   347→        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   348→        side = side / self.side_scale\n   349→        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n   350→        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n   351→\n   352→    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n   353→    def paw_velocity(self):\n   354→        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   355→        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   356→\n   357→    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n   358→    def motion_energy(self):\n   359→        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   360→        rows = []\n   361→        for trial in self.trials:\n   362→            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n   363→            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n   364→            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n   365→        return np.array(rows)\n   366→\n   367→    def outputs(self):\n   368→        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n   369→        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n   370→        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n   371→        energy = self.motion_energy()\n   372→        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n   373→                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n   374→                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n   375→\n   376→\n   377→# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n   378→# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n   379→# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n   380→# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n   381→\n   382→def _quality(cluster):\n   383→    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n   384→    label = cluster['quality']\n   385→    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n   386→\n   387→\n   388→class Neural:\n   389→    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n   390→\n   391→    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n   392→        self.obj = obj\n   393→        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n   394→        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n   395→                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n   396→                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n   397→\n   398→        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n   399→        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n   400→                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n   401→        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n   402→\n   403→    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n   404→        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n   405→        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n   406→        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n   407→            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n   408→            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n   409→                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n   410→        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n   411→\n   412→    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n   413→        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n   414→        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n   415→        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n   416→\n   417→        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n   418→        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n   419→        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n   420→        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n   421→\n   422→    def rates(self):\n   423→        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   424→        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n   425→        rates = []\n   426→        for cluster in self.clusters:\n   427→            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n   457→\n   458→    return {\n   459→        'session_id': name,\n   460→        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n   461→        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n   462→        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   463→        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   464→        'n_trials': n_trials,\n   465→        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n   466→    }\n   467→\n   468→\n   469→def assemble(results):\n   470→    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n   471→    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n   472→    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   473→\n   474→    return {\n   475→        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n   476→        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n   477→        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n   478→\n   479→        'subjects': subjects,\n   480→        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n   481→\n   482→        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n   483→        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n   484→\n   485→        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   486→        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   487→        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   488→\n   489→        'metadata': {\n   490→            'task_description':\n   491→                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. struct\n        return {k: _h5(f, node[k]) for k in node}\n    if node.attrs.get('MATLAB_class', b'').decode() == 'char':\n        return ''.join(chr(c) for c in np.asarray(node[()]).ravel())\n    if node.dtype == object:                                # Early-lick and photostim trials\n# are dropped, following the paper. The index keeps the original trial numbers, which\n# every later step uses to reach back into the raw arrays.\n\ndef trial_column(bp, *names):\n    \"\"\"One entry per trial from a field of bp, e.g. trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable').\"\"\"\n    n_trials = int(np.ravel(bp['Ntrials'])[0])\n    field = bp\n    for name in names:\n        field = field[name]\n    return np.ravel(np.asarray(field, float))[:n_trials]\n\n\ndef trial_info(bp):\n    \"\"\"Outcome, context, and lick direction per trial, without early-lick or photostim trials.\"\"\"\n    hit = trial_column(bp, 'hit') > 0\n    miss = trial_column(bp, 'miss') > 0\n    right = trial_column(bp, 'R') > 0\n    autowater = trial_column(bp, 'autowater') > 0\n    early = trial_column(bp, 'early') > 0\n    stim = trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable') > 0\n    n_trials = hit.size\n\n    # a hit is a correct lick and a miss an incorrect one; the animal ignored the rest\n    outcome = np.full(n_trials, OUTCOME['ignore'])\n    outcome[hit] = OUTCOME['correct']\n    outcome[miss] = OUTCOME['incorrect']\n\n    # water is given from a random port without any cue in the WC context\n    context = np.where(autowater, CONTEXT['WC'], CONTEXT['DR'])\n\n    # a hit licks the instructed port, a miss licks the other one, an ignore neither\n    lick_direction = np.full(n_trials, LICK['no lick'])\n    lick_direction[hit] = np.where(right[hit], LICK['right'], LICK['left'])\n    lick_direction[miss] = np.where(right[miss], LICK['left'], LICK['right'])\n\n    info = pd.DataFrame({'lick_direction': lick_direction, 'context': context,\n                         'outcome': outcome})\n    info.index.name = 'trial'\n    return info[~early & ~stim]\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- camera --\n# Tongue velocity, paw velocity, and motion energy for one session. A class because the\n# video offset, the per-view scales, and the median split are session-wide\n# quantities that every trial reuses.\n\ndef _feature(traj, index):\n    \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature, whichever way the file orders ts.\"\"\"\n    ts = np.asarray(traj['ts'])\n    if ts.shape[0] == len(traj['featNames']):      # v7.3 reads it as (features, xyl, frames)\n        return ts[index]\n    return ts[:, :, index].T                       # v5 keeps MATLAB's (frames, xyl, features)\n\n\ndef _runs(valid):\n    \"\"\"First and last+1 index of each contiguous run of valid frames.\"\"\"\n    edges = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], valid.astype(int), [0]]))\n    return zip(np.flatnonzero(edges == 1), np.flatnonzero(edges == -1))\n\n\ndef _bin_frames(time, values):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the valid frames in each bin, NaN where a bin holds no valid frame.\"\"\"\n    bin_of_frame = np.searchsorted(BIN_EDGES, time, side='right') - 1\n    valid = np.isfinite(values) & (bin_of_frame >= 0) & (bin_of_frame < N_BINS)\n    total = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], weights=values[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    count = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _mean_over_available(first, second):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the two, whichever one is there alone, or NaN where neither is.\"\"\"\n    stacked = np.stack([first, second])\n    count = np.sum(np.isfinite(stacked), axis=0)\n    total = np.nansum(stacked, axis=0)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _discretize(values, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Two classes split at the threshold; untracked bins take the trailing class.\"\"\"\n    code = np.where(values >= threshold, MOVEMENT['above threshold'], MOVEMENT['below threshold'])\n    return np.where(np.isnan(values), MOVEMENT['not visible'], code)\n\n\nclass Camera:\n    \"\"\"The three camera streams of a session, binned and cut into classes.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, name, trials, datadir):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.trials = list(trials)\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.offset = self._video_offset()             # one constant for the whole session\n        self.energy = load_motion_energy(name, datadir)\n\n        # frame resolution velocity, for the two tongue views and for the paw\n        self.side_tongue = self._trial_velocity(SIDE_TONGUE)\n        self.bottom_tongue = self._trial_velocity(BOTTOM_TONGUE)\n        self.paw = self._trial_velocity(PAW)\n\n        # the two cameras see the tongue at different pixel scales\n        self.side_scale = self._view_scale(self.side_tongue)\n        self.bottom_scale = self._view_scale(self.bottom_tongue)\n\n    def _trial_velocity(self, feature):\n        \"\"\"Frame resolution velocity of one tracked feature, one entry per trial.\"\"\"\n        return {trial: self._velocity(self._track(trial, feature)) for trial in self.trials}\n\n    def _video_offset(self):\n        \"\"\"Seconds by which the video clock leads the behavior clock (findVideoOffset.m).\"\"\"\n        sample_rate = float(np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['fs'])[0])\n        video_start = np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['bitcode']['bitstart']) / sample_rate\n        behavior_start = trial_column(self.obj['bp'], 'ev', 'bitStart')\n        return pd.Series(video_start).mode().iloc[0] - pd.Series(behavior_start).mode().iloc[0]\n\n    def _traj(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Tracking of one trial and camera, however the file stores it.\"\"\"\n        traj = self.obj['traj'][view]\n        if isinstance(traj, dict):                     # v7.3: one entry per camera\n            return {field: traj[field][trial] for field in traj}\n        entry = traj[trial]                            # v5: one entry per trial\n        if hasattr(entry, '_fieldnames'):              # scipy leaves nested structs as objects\n            return {field: getattr(entry, field) for field in entry._fieldnames}\n        return entry\n\n    # camera frame time after correcting for the video offset and aligning to the go cue, in seconds\n    def _frame_time(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Frame times of one trial and camera, in seconds from go cue onset.\"\"\"\n        frame_times = np.ravel(self._traj(trial, view)['frameTimes'])\n        return frame_times - self.offset - self.go_cue[trial]\n\n    def _camera_of(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"The camera that tracks a feature, and its tracking for this trial.\"\"\"\n        for view in (SIDE, BOTTOM):\n            traj = self._traj(trial, view)\n            if feature in list(traj['featNames']):     # the name picks out the camera\n                return view, traj\n        raise KeyError('%s is tracked by neither camera' % feature)\n\n    # x, y, and likelihood within the time window\n    def _track(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature over the frames inside the window.\"\"\"\n        view, traj = self._camera_of(trial, feature)\n        x, y, likelihood = _feature(traj, list(traj['featNames']).index(feature))\n        time = self._frame_time(trial, view)           # frame counts can differ between views\n        inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n\n    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n    def _velocity(self, track):\n        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n            return time, speed\n\n        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n\n        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n                continue\n            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n        return time, speed\n\n    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n\n    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n    def tongue_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        side = side / self.side_scale\n        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n\n    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n    def paw_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n\n    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n    def motion_energy(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for trial in self.trials:\n            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n        return np.array(rows)\n\n    def outputs(self):\n        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n        energy = self.motion_energy()\n        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n\ndef _quality(cluster):\n    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n    label = cluster['quality']\n    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n\n\nclass Neural:\n    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n\n        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n\n    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n\n    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n\n        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n\n    def rates(self):\n        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n        rates = []\n        for cluster in self.clusters:\n            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # counts -> Hz\n            if rate.mean() > MIN_RATE:\n                rates.append(gaussian_filter1d(rate, sigma, axis=1, mode='reflect'))\n        return np.asarray(rates, np.float32)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ session --\n\ndef process_session(name, datadir):\n    \"\"\"Convert one session into per-trial neural, input, and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    obj = load_mat(session_file(name, 'data_structure', datadir))\n    info = trial_info(obj['bp'])\n\n    neural = Neural(obj, SESSIONS[name], info.index)\n    info = info.loc[neural.trials]                 # drops any trial past the recording\n    n_trials = len(info)\n\n    rates = neural.rates()\n    tongue, paw, energy = Camera(obj, name, info.index, datadir).outputs()\n\n    # the three per-trial values are repeated across bins so all six outputs share one array\n    out = np.empty((n_trials, len(OUTPUT_NAMES), N_BINS), np.int8)\n    out[:, 0, :] = info['lick_direction'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 1, :] = info['context'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 2, :] = info['outcome'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 3, :] = tongue\n    out[:, 4, :] = paw\n    out[:, 5, :] = energy\n\n    inp = np.tile(TIME.astype(np.float32), (n_trials, 1, 1))       # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n\n    return {\n        'session_id': name,\n        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'n_trials': n_trials,\n        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n    }\n\n\ndef assemble(results):\n    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    return {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n\n        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description':\n                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. In the delayed-response '\n                'context a tone instructs the direction, a delay follows, and an auditory '\n                'go cue opens the response window; in the water-cued context all cues are '\n                'omitted and water arrives at a random port. Decoded variables: lick '\n                'direction, behavioral context, outcome, and tongue, paw, and whole-body '\n                'movement.',\n            'time_bin_size': BIN * 1000.0,          # ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'go cue onset (auditory chirp, 10 ms)',\n            'off_start': T_START,\n            'off_end': T_STOP,\n\n            'dataset': 'Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nat Neurosci 2024, ALM recordings',\n            'neural_units': 'Hz',\n            'session_selection':\n                \"The 44 sessions listed in the author's DataLoadingScripts/Recording and \"\n                'video/load<ANM>_ALMVideo.m that are present in data/, with the probe each '\n                'entry specifies; two-probe sessions are concatenated.',\n            'unit_curation':\n                'Cluster quality labels, lower cased, excluding %s, then units whose mean '\n                'rate over the window is at or below %.1f Hz.'\n                % (', '.join(sorted(QUALITY_DROP)), MIN_RATE),\n            'trial_curation': 'Early-lick and photostimulation trials excluded.',\n            'neural_processing':\n                'Spikes counted in %.0f ms bins, converted to Hz, smoothed with a gaussian '\n                'of %d ms standard deviation.' % (BIN * 1000, RATE_SD_MS),\n            'movement_discretisation':\n                'Tongue and paw velocity are the speed of the DeepLabCut position after a '\n                '%d ms gaussian, computed within each run of frames whose likelihood '\n                'exceeds %.1f. The two tongue views are each divided by their %dth '\n                'percentile before being averaged. Each stream is averaged into %.0f ms '\n                'bins and split at its own session %dth percentile; bins the camera did '\n                'not track take the trailing \"not visible\" class.'\n                % (SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS, LIKELIHOOD_CUT, NORMALIZE_PCT, BIN * 1000, SPLIT_PCT),\n            'session_info': [\n                {'session_id': r['session_id'], 'subject': r['subject'],\n                 'n_trials': r['n_trials'], 'n_units': r['n_units']}\n                for r in results\n            ],\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile', help='output pickle path')\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='process all sessions (default)')\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--datadir', default=DATA_DIR,\n                    help='directory holding the Ephys_Behavior/ folders (default: %(default)s)')\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    names = SAMPLE if args.sample else list(SESSIONS)\n    print('%d sessions' % len(names), flush=True)\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    results = []\n    for i, name in enumerate(names, 1):\n        t1 = time.time()\n        res = process_session(name, args.datadir)\n        results.append(res)\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        print('[%2d/%d] %-18s %4d trials %4d units  %5.1fs  (elapsed %5.1fs, total ~%5.1fs)'\n              % (i, len(names), name, res['n_trials'], res['n_units'],\n                 time.time() - t1, elapsed, elapsed / i * len(names)), flush=True)\n\n    data = assemble(results)\n    print('\\nconverted in %.1f s' % (time.time() - t0), flush=True)\n    print('sessions %d | subjects %d | trials %d | units %d'\n          % (len(results), len(data['subjects']),\n             sum(r['n_trials'] for r in results), sum(r['n_units'] for r in results)),\n          flush=True)\n\n    with open(args.outfile, 'wb') as f:\n        pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)\n    print('wrote %s (%.1f GB)'\n          % (args.outfile, os.path.getsize(args.outfile) / 1e9), flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","numLines":571,"startLine":1,"totalLines":571}}}

Low-level helpers\n   143→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   144→def data_path(anm, date, task):\n   145→    return os.path.join(DATA_DIR[task], f\"data_structure_{anm}_{date}.mat\")\n   146→\n   147→\n   148→def me_path(anm, date, task):\n   149→    return os.path.join(DATA_DIR[task], f\"motionEnergy_{anm}_{date}.mat\")\n   150→\n   151→\n   152→def load_obj(anm, date, task):\n   153→    \"\"\"loadObjs.m -- read one session's data object (handles MAT v7 and v7.3).\"\"\"\n   154→    from pymatreader import read_mat\n   155→    return read_mat(data_path(anm, date, task))[\"obj\"]\n   156→\n   157→\n   158→def vec(x):\n   159→    \"\"\"Flatten a MATLAB numeric field to a 1-D float array.\"\"\"\n   160→    return np.ravel(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))\n   161→\n   162→\n   163→def mode_of(a):\n   164→    \"\"\"MATLAB mode() for a numeric vector (smallest most-frequent value), NaNs dropped.\"\"\"\n   165→    a = a[~np.isnan(a)]\n   166→    vals, counts = np.unique(a, return_counts=True)\n   167→    return float(vals[np.argmax(counts)])\n   168→\n   169→\n   170→def causal_gaussian(n=SMOOTH_N, alpha=2.5):\n   171→    \"\"\"mySmooth.m kernel: gausswin(n) with the first floor(n/2) taps zeroed, normalised.\"\"\"\n   172→    k = np.arange(n)\n   173→    w = np.exp(-0.5 * (alpha * (k - (n - 1) / 2.0) / ((n - 1) / 2.0)) ** 2)\n   174→    w[: n // 2] = 0.0\n   175→    return w / w.sum()\n   176→\n   177→\n   178→KERNEL = causal_gaussian()\n   179→\n   180→\n   181→def my_smooth(x, kern=KERNEL, bctype=BCTYPE):\n   182→    \"\"\"mySmooth(x, N, bctype) for x of shape (T, M); operates on the first dimension.\n   183→\n   184→    Reproduces `conv(..., 'same')` with an odd, causal kernel: because taps 0..n//2-1 are\n   185→    zero, the 'same' convolution reduces to a causal FIR filter with n - n//2 taps, which\n   186→    is evaluated directly (exact, no FFT round-off).\n   187→    \"\"\"\n   188→    n = kern.size\n   189→    half = (n - 1) // 2\n   190→    if bctype == \"reflect\":\n   191→        xp = np.concatenate([x[:n], x], axis=0)\n   192→        trim = n\n   193→    elif bctype == \"zeropad\":\n   194→        xp = np.concatenate([np.zeros((n,) + x.shape[1:], dtype=x.dtype), x], axis=0)\n   195→        trim = n\n   196→    else:\n   197→        xp, trim = x, 0\n   198→    out = np.zeros_like(xp, dtype=np.float64)\n   199→    # conv(...,'same')[t] = sum_k xp[t + half - k] * kern[k]; kern[k]==0 for k < half\n   200→    for k in range(half, n):\n   201→        w = kern[k]\n   202→        if w == 0.0:\n   203→            continue\n   204→        j = k - half                      # delay in samples (>= 0)\n   205→        if j == 0:\n   206→            out += w * xp\n   207→        else:\n   208→            out[j:] += w * xp[:-j]\n   209→    return out[trim:]\n   210→\n   211→\n   212→def bin_index(t):\n   213→    \"\"\"histc(t, EDGES) bin index; -1 (or >= NT) when outside the analysis window.\"\"\"\n   214→    idx = np.searchsorted(EDGES, t, side=\"right\") - 1\n   215→    idx[(idx < 0) | (idx >= NT)] = -1\n   216→    return idx\n   217→\n   218→\n   219→def video_shift(obj):\n   220→    \"\"\"findVideoOffset.m -- seconds to subtract from traj frameTimes to get trial time.\"\"\"\n   221→    bit_start = mode_of(vec(obj[\"bp\"][\"ev\"][\"bitStart\"]))\n   222→    fs = float(vec(obj[\"sglx\"][\"fs\"])[0])\n   223→    vid_file_offset = mode_of(vec(obj[\"sglx\"][\"bitcode\"][\"bitstart\"])) / fs\n   224→    return vid_file_offset - bit_start\n   225→\n   226→\n   227→def as_cell_list(x, n):\n   228→    \"\"\"Normalise a MATLAB cell-array field to a python list with n entries.\n   229→\n   230→    pymatreader returns a list for a cell array with >1 element but the bare content\n   231→    when the cell array has exactly one element, so single-trial / single-cluster\n   232→    fields have to be wrapped.\n   233→    \"\"\"\n   234→    if isinstance(x, list):\n   235→        return x\n   236→    return [x] * n if n == 1 else [x] + [None] * (n - 1)\n   237→\n   238→\n   239→# kept for readability at the call sites\n   240→as_trial_list = as_cell_list\n   241→\n   242→\n   243→def frame_times(obj, view, itrial):\n   244→    \"\"\"frameTimes for one trial, or None when the video timing is unusable.\"\"\"\n   245→    ft = obj[\"traj\"][view][\"frameTimes\"]\n   246→    ft = ft[itrial] if isinstance(ft, list) else ft\n   247→    if ft is None:\n   248→        return None\n   249→    a = np.ravel(np.asarray(ft, dtype=np.float64))\n   250→    if a.size < 2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):\n   251→        return None\n   252→    return a\n   253→\n   254→\n   255→def load_motion_energy(anm, date, task, obj, ntrials):\n   256→    \"\"\"loadMotionEnergy.m -- list of per-trial 400 Hz motion-energy traces (or None).\"\"\"\n   257→    from pymatreader import read_mat\n   258→\n   259→    me = None\n   260→    p = me_path(anm, date, task)\n   261→    if os.path.exists(p):\n   262→        me = read_mat(p)[\"me\"]\n   263→    elif \"me\" in obj:\n   264→        me = obj[\"me\"]\n   265→    if me is None:\n   266→        return [None] * ntrials\n   267→    if isinstance(me, dict):                      # struct with .data (+ .moveThresh)\n   268→        me = me[\"data\"]\n   269→        if isinstance(me, dict):                  # me.data.data (older objects)\n   270→            me = me[\"data\"]\n   271→    me = as_trial_list(me, ntrials)\n   272→    out = []\n   273→    for i in range(ntrials):\n   274→        if i >= len(me) or me[i] is None:\n   275→            out.append(None)\n   276→            continue\n   277→        a = np.ravel(np.asarray(me[i], dtype=np.float64))\n   278→        out.append(a if a.size > 1 else None)\n   279→    return out\n   280→\n   281→\n   282→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   283→# Curation (findClusters.m / findTrials.m conditions)\n   284→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   285→def select_clusters(obj, probes):\n   286→    \"\"\"findClusters(quality, {'all'}): keep everything except garbage / noisy clusters.\n   287→\n   288→    Returns a list of (probe_index, cluster_index) for the designated ALM probe(s).\n   289→    The label comparison is case-sensitive, exactly as in the MATLAB `ismember` calls.\n   290→    \"\"\"\n   291→    clu = obj[\"clu\"]\n   292→    if isinstance(clu, dict):\n   293→        clu = [clu]\n   294→    keep = []\n   295→    for p in probes:\n   296→        c = clu[p - 1]\n   297→        if not isinstance(c, dict) or \"quality\" not in c:\n   298→            continue\n   299→        quals = c[\"quality\"]\n   300→        if not isinstance(quals, list):\n   301→            quals = [quals]\n   302→        for i, q in enumerate(quals):\n   303→            q = str(q).strip() if isinstance(q, (str, np.str_)) else \"\"\n   304→            if q not in QUALITY_REJECT:\n   305→                keep.append((p - 1, i))\n   306→    return keep\n   307→\n   308→\n   309→def trials_with_ephys(obj, probes, ntrials):\n   310→    \"\"\"Boolean mask of trials for which spikes were actually recorded.\n   311→\n   312→    In a few sessions the SpikeGLX recording stops before the behavioural session does\n   313→    (JEB24 2023-10-23 and 2023-11-03 lose the last ~30 trials, JEB12 2022-05-12 loses one\n   314→    trial, which `obj.trials.bp.haveEphys` also flags).  Those trials carry an all-zero\n   315→    neural matrix, which is not data, so they are dropped.  Detection uses *every* cluster\n   316→    on the probe, including the ones the quality filter rejects, so a trial is only\n   317→    declared ephys-less when literally no spike was sorted on it.\n   318→    \"\"\"\n   319→    clu = obj[\"clu\"]\n   320→    if isinstance(clu, dict):\n   321→        clu = [clu]\n   322→    has = np.zeros(ntrials, dtype=bool)\n   323→    for p in probes:\n   324→        c = clu[p - 1]\n   325→        if not isinstance(c, dict) or \"trial\" not in c:\n   326→            continue\n   327→        trialno = c[\"trial\"] if isinstance(c[\"trial\"], list) else [c[\"trial\"]]\n   328→        for t in trialno:\n   329→            if t is None:\n   330→                continue\n   331→            a = np.ravel(np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64))\n   332→            a = a[np.isfinite(a)].astype(np.int64) - 1\n   333→            a = a[(a >= 0) & (a < ntrials)]\n   334→            has[a] = True\n   335→    return has\n   336→\n   337→\n   338→def select_trials(bp, obj=None, probes=()):\n   339→    \"\"\"Trial curation.\n   340→\n   341→    - `findTrials` conditions `~stim.enable & ~early`: drop photoinactivation and\n   342→      early-lick trials (Methods: early-lick trials \"were omitted from analyses\").\n   343→    - drop trials with no electrophysiology (see `trials_with_ephys`).\n   344→    hit / miss / ignore and DR / WC trials are all kept, as the decoder outputs require.\n   345→    \"\"\"\n   346→    n = int(vec(bp[\"Ntrials\"])[0])\n   347→    early = vec(bp[\"early\"])[:n] > 0\n   348→    stim = vec(bp[\"stim\"][\"enable\"])[:n] > 0\n   349→    keep = ~(early | stim)\n   350→    if obj is not None:\n   351→        keep &= trials_with_ephys(obj, probes, n)\n   352→    return np.flatnonzero(keep), n\n   353→\n   354→\n   355→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   356→# Neural\n   357→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   358→def bin_and_smooth(obj, cluster_ids, align_times, ntrials):\n   359→    \"\"\"getSeq.m -- (ntrials, nclusters, NT) smoothed firing rates in spikes/s.\"\"\"\n   360→    clu = obj[\"clu\"]\n   361→    if isinstance(clu, dict):\n   362→        clu = [clu]\n   363→    counts = np.zeros((len(cluster_ids), ntrials, NT), dtype=np.float32)\n   364→    for ci, (p, i) in enumerate(cluster_ids):\n   365→        c = clu[p]\n   366→        trialtm = c[\"trialtm\"] if isinstance(c[\"trialtm\"], list) else [c[\"trialtm\"]]\n   367→        trialno = c[\"trial\"] if isinstance(c[\"trial\"], list) else [c[\"trial\"]]\n   368→        tm = np.ravel(np.asarray(trialtm[i], dtype=np.float64))\n   369→        tr = np.ravel(np.asarray(trialno[i], dtype=np.float64)).astype(np.int64) - 1\n   370→        ok = (tr >= 0) & (tr < ntrials) & np.isfinite(tm)\n   371→        tm, tr = tm[ok], tr[ok]\n   372→        if tm.size == 0:\n   373→            continue\n   374→        aligned = tm - align_times[tr]              # alignSpikes.m\n   375→        b = bin_index(aligned)\n   376→        ok = b >= 0\n   377→        if not np.any(ok):\n   378→            continue\n   379→        flat = tr[ok] * NT + b[ok]\n   380→        counts[ci] = np.bincount(flat, minlength=ntrials * NT).reshape(ntrials, NT)\n   381→    rate = counts / DT                               # spikes/s\n   382→    # smooth along time: my_smooth wants (T, M)\n   383→    m = rate.reshape(-1, NT).T                       # (NT, nclu*ntrials)\n   384→    m = my_smooth(m)\n   385→    return m.T.reshape(len(cluster_ids), ntrials, NT)\n   386→\n   387→\n   388→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   389→# Video-derived outputs\n   390→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   391→def interp_trace(src_t, src_y, taxis):\n   392→    \"\"\"interp1(src_t, src_y, taxis) -- linear, NaN outside the source range.\"\"\"\n   393→    return np.interp(taxis, src_t, src_y, left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n   394→\n   395→\n   396→def interp_feature(ts, featix, ft, align_time, taxis):\n   397→    \"\"\"findPosition.m -- interpolate one DLC feature's (x, y) onto the aligned time base.\"\"\"\n   398→    src_t = ft - align_time\n   399→    x = interp_trace(src_t, ts[:, 0, featix], taxis)\n   400→    y = interp_trace(src_t, ts[:, 1, featix], taxis)\n   401→    return x, y\n   402→\n   403→\n   404→def feature_speed(x, y, subtract_baseline):\n   405→    \"\"\"findVelocity.m -- |d(pos)/dbin| at every timepoint where the feature is visible.\n   406→\n   407→    The derivative is evaluated separately on each contiguous visible run (np.gradient,\n   408→    i.e. MATLAB's gradient()), so a gap never contaminates a neighbouring sample.\n   409→    Returns NaN where the feature is not visible.\n   410→    \"\"\"\n   411→    visible = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)\n   412→    vx = np.full(x.shape, np.nan)\n   413→    vy = np.full(y.shape, np.nan)\n   414→    idx = np.flatnonzero(visible)\n   415→    if idx.size == 0:\n   416→        return np.full(x.shape, np.nan)\n   417→    brk = np.flatnonzero(np.diff(idx) > 1)\n   418→    starts = np.concatenate(([0], brk + 1))\n   419→    stops = np.concatenate((brk, [idx.size - 1]))\n   420→    for s, e in zip(starts, stops):\n   421→        seg = idx[s:e + 1]\n   422→        if seg.size == 1:\n   423→            vx[seg] = 0.0\n   424→            vy[seg] = 0.0\n   425→        else:\n   426→            vx[seg] = np.gradient(x[seg])\n   427→            vy[seg] = np.gradient(y[seg])\n   428→    if subtract_baseline:\n   429→        # findVelocity.m: basederiv = median(diff(pos),'omitnan'); xvel/yvel -= basederiv(1)\n   430→        d = np.diff(np.stack([x, y], axis=1), axis=0)\n   431→        with warnings.catch_warnings():\n   432→            warnings.simplefilter(\"ignore\")\n   433→            base = np.nanmedian(d, axis=0)\n   434→        if np.isfinite(base[0]):\n   435→            vx = vx - base[0]\n   436→            vy = vy - base[0]\n   437→    return np.hypot(vx, vy)\n   438→\n   439→\n   440→def feature_index(obj, view, name):\n   441→    \"\"\"findDLCFeatIndex.m -- index of a DLC feature in obj.traj{view}.featNames.\n   442→\n   443→    Scans trials until one has a usable featNames list (early trials can hold dummy data).\n   444→    \"\"\"\n   445→    fn = obj[\"traj\"][view][\"featNames\"]\n   446→    cands = fn if isinstance(fn, list) else [fn]\n   447→    for names in cands:\n   448→        if names is None:\n   449→            continue\n   450→        names = [str(s) for s in np.ravel(np.asarray(names, dtype=object))]\n   451→        if name in names:\n   452→            return names.index(name)\n   453→    raise ValueError(f\"DLC feature {name!r} not found in view {view + 1}\")\n   454→\n   455→\n   456→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   457→# Per-session conversion\n   458→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   459→def process_session(args):\n   460→    anm, date, probes, task = args\n   461→    t0 = time.time()\n   462→    obj = load_obj(anm, date, task)\n   463→    t_load = time.time() - t0\n   464→\n   465→    bp = obj[\"bp\"]\n   466→    trials, ntrials_all = select_trials(bp, obj, probes)\n   467→    gocue = vec(bp[\"ev\"][ALIGN_EVENT])[:ntrials_all]\n   468→\n   469→    # ---- neural -----------------------------------------------------------------\n   470→    t1 = time.time()\n   471→    cluster_ids = select_clusters(obj, probes)\n   472→    rates = bin_and_smooth(obj, cluster_ids, gocue, ntrials_all)      # (nclu, ntr, NT)\n   473→    rates = rates[:, trials, :]\n   474→    n_quality = len(cluster_ids)\n   475→    if n_quality:\n   476→        mean_fr = rates.reshape(n_quality, -1).mean(axis=1)           # removeLowFRClusters.m\n   477→        keep_unit = mean_fr > LOW_FR\n   478→        rates = rates[keep_unit]\n   479→    else:\n   480→        keep_unit = np.zeros(0, dtype=bool)\n   481→    nunits = rates.shape[0]\n   482→    t_neural = time.time() - t1\n   483→\n   484→    # ---- per-trial outputs -------------------------------------------------------\n   485→    R = vec(bp[\"R\"])[:ntrials_all] > 0\n   486→    L = vec(bp[\"L\"])[:ntrials_all] > 0\n   487→    hit = vec(bp[\"hit\"])[:ntrials_all] > 0\n   488→    miss = vec(bp[\"miss\"])[:ntrials_all] > 0\n   489→    no = vec(bp[\"no\"])[:ntrials_all] > 0\n   490→    autowater = vec(bp[\"autowater\"])[:ntrials_all] > 0\n   491→\n   492→    lick = np.full(ntrials_all, 2, dtype=np.int8)                     # 2 = none\n   493→    lick[(L & hit) | (R & miss)] = 0                                  # left\n   494→    lick[(R & hit) | (L & miss)] = 1                                  # right\n   495→    lick[no] = 2                                                      # getPrevChoice: NaN on ignore\n   496→    context = np.where(autowater, 0, 1).astype(np.int8)               # 0 = WC, 1 = DR\n   497→    outcome = np.full(ntrials_all, 0, dtype=np.int8)                  # 0 = incorrect\n   498→    outcome[hit] = 1                                                  # 1 = correct\n   499→    outcome[no] = 2                                                   # 2 = ignore\n   500→\n   501→    # ---- video-derived, time-varying outputs -------------------------------------\n   502→    t2 = time.time()\n   503→    vidshift = video_shift(obj)\n   504→    taxis = TIME + ADVANCE_MOVEMENT\n   505→    me_traces = load_motion_energy(anm, date, task, obj, ntrials_all)\n   506→    ts_side = as_trial_list(obj[\"traj\"][TONGUE_VIEW][\"ts\"], ntrials_all)\n   507→    ts_bot = as_trial_list(obj[\"traj\"][PAW_VIEW][\"ts\"], ntrials_all)\n   508→    i_tongue = feature_index(obj, TONGUE_VIEW, TONGUE_FEAT)\n   509→    i_paws = [feature_index(obj, PAW_VIEW, f) for f in PAW_FEATS]\n   510→\n   511→    ntr = trials.size\n   512→    tongue_v = np.full((ntr, NT), np.nan)\n   513→    paw_v = np.full((ntr, NT), np.nan)\n   514→    me_v = np.full((ntr, NT), np.nan)\n   515→    for k, it in enumerate(trials):\n   516→        align = gocue[it] + vidshift\n   517→        ft_side = frame_times(obj, TONGUE_VIEW, it)\n   518→        ft_bot = frame_times(obj, PAW_VIEW, it)\n   519→        if ft_side is not None:\n   520→            ts = np.asarray(ts_side[it], dtype=np.float64)\n   521→            if ts.ndim == 3 and ts.shape[0] == ft_side.size:\n   522→                x, y = interp_feature(ts, i_tongue, ft_side, align, taxis)\n   523→                tongue_v[k] = feature_speed(x, y, subtract_baseline=False)\n   524→        if ft_bot is not None:\n   525→            ts = np.asarray(ts_bot[it], dtype=np.float64)\n   526→            if ts.ndim == 3 and ts.shape[0] == ft_bot.size:\n   527→                sp = []\n   528→                for ip in i_paws:\n   529→                    x, y = interp_feature(ts, ip, ft_bot, align, taxis)\n   530→                    sp.append(feature_speed(x, y, subtract_baseline=True))\n   531→                sp = np.stack(sp, axis=0)\n   532→                with warnings.catch_warnings():\n   533→                    warnings.simplefilter(\"ignore\")\n   534→                    paw_v[k] = np.nanmean(sp, axis=0)   # mean over the visible paw(s)\n   535→        tr = me_traces[it]\n   536→        if tr is not None and ft_side is not None and tr.size == ft_side.size:\n   537→            me_v[k] = interp_trace(ft_side - align, tr, taxis)\n   538→    t_video = time.time() - t2\n   539→\n   540→    # ---- discretise with the per-session 50th percentile --------------------------\n   541→    def discretise(v):\n   542→        valid = np.isfinite(v)\n   543→        out = np.full(v.shape, 2, dtype=np.int8)\n   544→        if valid.any():\n   545→            thr = float(np.median(v[valid]))\n   546→            out[valid] = (v[valid] >= thr).astype(np.int8)\n   547→        else:\n   548→            thr = np.nan\n   549→        return out, thr\n   550→\n   551→    tongue_c, thr_tongue = discretise(tongue_v)\n   552→    paw_c, thr_paw = discretise(paw_v)\n   553→    me_c, thr_me = discretise(me_v)\n   554→\n   555→    # ---- assemble ----------------------------------------------------------------\n   556→    neural, inputs, outputs = [], [], []\n   557→    input_trial = np.ascontiguousarray(TIME[None, :], dtype=np.float32)   # shared, read-only\n   558→    for k, it in enumerate(trials):\n   559→        neural.append(np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, k, :], dtype=np.float32))\n   560→        inputs.append(input_trial)\n   561→        out = np.empty((6, NT), dtype=np.int8)\n   562→        out[0] = lick[it]\n   563→        out[1] = context[it]\n   564→        out[2] = outcome[it]\n   565→        out[3] = tongue_c[k]\n   566→        out[4] = paw_c[k]\n   567→        out[5] = me_c[k]\n   568→        outputs.append(out)\n   569→\n   570→    info = dict(\n   571→        anm=anm, date=date, task=task, probes=list(probes),\n   572→        session=f\"{anm}_{date}\",\n   573→        two_context=(anm, date) in TWO_CONTEXT,\n   574→        ntrials_raw=ntrials_all, ntrials_kept=int(ntr),\n   575→        trial_index=trials.astype(np.int32),   # 0-based index into the raw session's trials\n   576→        cluster_index=np.array([(p + 1, i) for p, i in cluster_ids], dtype=np.int32\n   577→                               )[keep_unit] if n_quality else np.zeros((0, 2), np.int32),\n   578→        n_units_quality=n_quality, n_units=int(nunits),\n   579→        n_trials_early=int(np.sum(vec(bp[\"early\"])[:ntrials_all] > 0)),\n   580→        n_trials_stim=int(np.sum(vec(bp[\"stim\"][\"enable\"])[:ntrials_all] > 0)),\n   581→        n_trials_no_ephys=int(np.sum(~trials_with_ephys(obj, probes, ntrials_all))),\n   582→        n_autowater_kept=int(np.sum(autowater[trials])),\n   583→        vidshift=float(vidshift),\n   584→        thr_tongue=thr_tongue, thr_paw=thr_paw, thr_me=thr_me,\n   585→        timing=dict(load=t_load, neural=t_neural, video=t_video,\n   586→                    total=time.time() - t0),\n   587→    )\n   588→    return dict(neural=neural, input=inputs, output=outputs, info=info)\n   589→\n   590→\n   591→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   592→# Diagnostic plots (--show-processing)\n   593→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   594→def show_processing(args, result, outdir=\"/app\"):\n   595→    \"\"\"Plot every processing step for one session so the conversion can be eyeballed.\"\"\"\n   596→    import matplotlib\n   597→    matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n   598→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   599→\n   600→    anm, date, probes, gocue = vec(bp[\"ev\"][ALIGN_EVENT])[:ntrials_all]\n   606→    cluster_ids = select_clusters(obj, probes)\n   607→    vidshift = video_shift(obj)\n   608→\n   609→    neural = result[\"neural\"]\n   610→    outputs = result[\"output\"]\n   611→    info = result[\"info\"]\n   612→    nunits = neural[0].shape[0]\n   613→\n   614→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(20, 20))\n   615→\n   616→    # (1) raw spike raster + binned + smoothed rate for one unit / one trial ------------\n   617→    k = min(5, len(trials) - 1)\n   618→    it = trials[k]\n   619→    clu = obj[\"clu\"]\n   620→    if --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   751→# Main\n   752→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   753→def main():\n   754→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n   755→    ap.add_argument(\"outfile\", help=\"output pickle path\")\n   756→    ap.add_argument(\"--full\", action=\"store_true\", default=True, help=\"process all sessions\")\n   757→    ap.add_argument(\"--sample\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"process only 2 sessions\")\n   758→    ap.add_argument(\"--show-processing\", action=\"store_true\",\n   759→                    help=\"plot every processing step for up to 2 sessions\")\n   760→    ap.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=12)\n   761→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   762→\n   763→    sessions = SESSIONS\n   764→    if args.sample:\n   765→        # one two-context fixed-delay session (JEB19 2023-04-18, has both DR and WC blocks)\n   766→        # and one randomized-delay session (JEB11 2022-05-10)\n   767→        sessions = [SESSIONS[8], SESSIONS[25]]\n   768→    print(f\"Converting {len(sessions)} session(s) with {args.workers} workers\", flush=True)\n   769→\n   770→    t_start = time.time()\n   771→    results = []\n   772→    if args.workers > 1 and len(sessions) > 1:\n   773→        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as ex:\n   774→            for r in ex.map(process_session, sessions):\n   775→                results.append(r)\n   776→                i = r[\"info\"]\n   777→                print(f\"  [{len(results):2d}/{len(sessions)}] {i['session']:>18s} \"\n   778→                      f\"({i['task'][:5]}) units {i['n_units']:4d}/{i['n_units_quality']:4d}  \"\n   779→                      f\"trials {i['ntrials_kept']:4d}/{i['ntrials_raw']:4d}  \"\n   780→                      f\"t={i['timing']['total']:.1f}s \"\n   781→                      f\"(load {i['timing']['load']:.1f}, neural {i['timing']['neural']:.1f}, \"\n   782→                      f\"video {i['timing']['video']:.1f})\", flush=True)\n   783→    else:\n   784→        for s in sessions:\n   785→            r = process_session(s)\n   786→            results.append(r)\n   787→            i = r[\"info\"]\n   788→            print(f\"  [{len(results):2d}/{len(sessions)}] {i['session']:>18s} \"\n   789→                  f\"({i['task'][:5]}) units {i['n_units']:4d}/{i['n_units_quality']:4d}  \"\n   790→                  f\"trials {i['ntrials_kept']:4d}/{i['ntrials_raw']:4d}  \"\n   791→                  f\"t={i['timing']['total']:.1f}s \"\n   792→                  f\"(load {i['timing']['load']:.1f}, neural {i['timing']['neural']:.1f}, \"\n   793→                  f\"video {i['timing']['video']:.1f})\", flush=True)\n   794→    t_proc = time.time() - t_start\n   795→    print(f\"Processing took {t_proc:.1f} s \"\n   796→          f\"({t_proc / max(len(sessions), 1):.1f} s/session)\", flush=True)\n   797→\n   798→    # ---- session-level curation ------------------------------------------------------\n   799→    kept, dropped = [], []\n   800→    for s, r in zip(sessions, results):\n   801→        i = r[\"info\"]\n   802→        if i[\"n_units\"] < MIN_UNITS:\n   803→            dropped.append((i[\"session\"], f\"only {i['n_units']} units (< {MIN_UNITS})\"))\n   804→        elif i[\"ntrials_kept\"] < 2:\n   805→            dropped.append((i[\"session\"], f\"only {i['ntrials_kept']} trials\"))\n   806→        else:\n   807→            kept.append((s, r))\n   808→    for sid, why in dropped:\n   809→        print(f\"  DROPPED session {sid}: {why}\", flush=True)\n   810→\n   811→    # ---- assemble the dataset dict ---------------------------------------------------\n   812→    subjects = sorted({r[\"info\"][\"anm\"] for _, r in kept})\n   813→    data = {\n   814→        \"neural\": [r[\"neural\"] for _, r in kept],\n   815→        \"input\": [r[\"input\"] for _, r in kept],\n   816→        \"output\": [r[\"output\"] for _, r in kept],\n   817→        \"subjects\": subjects,\n   818→        \"subject_idx\": np.array([subjects.index(r[\"info\"][\"anm\"]) for _, r in kept],\n   819→                                dtype=np.int64),\n   820→        \"brain_regions\": list(BRAIN_REGIONS),\n   821→        \"brain_region_idx\": [np.zeros(r[\"neural\"][0].shape[0], dtype=np.int64)\n   822→                             for _, r in kept],\n   823→        \"input_names\": list(INPUT_NAMES),\n   824→        \"output_names\": list(OUTPUT_NAMES),\n   825→        \"output_values\": [list(v) for v in OUTPUT_VALUES],\n   826→        \"metadata\": {\n   827→            \"task_description\":\n   828→                \"Head-fixed mice performed two directional-licking tasks that alternated \"\n   829→                \"block-wise within a session: a delayed-response (DR) task, in which an \"\n   830→                \"auditory tone indicated the rewarded lickport and an auditory go cue \"\n   831→                \"(after a delay) instructed movement, and a water-cued (WC) task, in which \"\n   832→                \"all auditory cues were omitted and a water drop was delivered at a random \"\n   833→                \"time from a randomly chosen lickport. A subset of sessions used a \"\n   834→                \"randomized delay duration (0.3-3.6 s). Neural activity is extracellular \"\n   835→                \"spiking recorded in anterior lateral motor cortex (ALM) with silicon \"\n   836→                \"probes. The decoder predicts, from ALM activity plus the time relative to \"\n   837→                \"the go cue: the direction of the animal's lick (left/right/none), the \"\n   838→                \"behavioural context (WC/DR), the trial outcome \"\n   839→                \"(incorrect/correct/ignore), and three discretised measures of uninstructed \"\n   840→                \"movement from high-speed video (tongue velocity, paw velocity and whole-\"\n   841→                \"frame motion energy, each split at that session's 50th percentile with a \"\n   842→                \"third class for timepoints where the feature/video is unavailable).\",\n   843→            \"time_bin_size\": DT * 1000.0,\n   844→            \"temporal_alignment_event\":\n   845→                \"go cue onset (bp.ev.goCue; in water-cued trials this is the time the water \"\n   846→                \"drop is presented)\",\n   847→            \"off_start\": TMIN,\n   848→            \"off_end\": TMAX,\n   849→            \"time_bin_centers_s\": TIME.astype(np.float32),\n   850→            \"neural_units\": \"spikes/s (spike counts in 10 ms bins, divided by the bin width \"\n   851→                            \"and smoothed with a causal Gaussian kernel, gausswin(15), \"\n   852→                            \"'reflect' boundary) -- identical to obj.trialdat in the \"\n   853→                            \"reference MATLAB pipeline\",\n   854→            \"smoothing\": {\"kernel\": \"causal gaussian (gausswin(15), first 7 taps zeroed)\",\n   855→                          \"window_samples\": SMOOTH_N, \"boundary\": BCTYPE},\n   856→            \"neuron_curation\": \"ALM probe(s) as designated in the reference \"\n   857→                               \"load<ANM>_ALMVideo.m scripts; clusters labelled \"\n   858→                               \"garbage/gabrga/noisy/real? removed (findClusters 'all'); \"\n   859→                               f\"mean firing rate > {LOW_FR} Hz over the analysis window \"\n   860→                               \"(removeLowFRClusters).\",\n   861→            \"trial_curation\": \"early-lick trials (bp.early) and photoinactivation trials \"\n   862→                              \"(bp.stim.enable) removed, as in every params.condition of the \"\n   863→                              \"reference code; hit / miss / ignore and DR / WC trials kept.\",\n   864→            \"session_curation\": f\"sessions with >= {MIN_UNITS} curated units (Methods).\",\n   865→            \"video\": {\"frame_rate_hz\": 400.0,\n   866→                      \"alignment\": \"frameTimes - videoOffset - goCue, with videoOffset from \"\n   867→                                   \"findVideoOffset.m; linear interpolation onto the 10 ms \"\n   868→                                   \"neural time base; no neural/video lag \"\n   869→                                   \"(params.advance_movement = 0)\",\n   870→                      \"tongue_feature\": f\"traj view 1 (side camera) '{TONGUE_FEAT}'\",\n   871→                      \"paw_feature\": \"traj view 2 (bottom camera), mean speed over the \"\n   872→                                     \"visible subset of ('top_paw', 'bottom_paw')\"},\n   873→            \"discretization\": \"per session, the 50th percentile of the valid (visible / \"\n   874→                              \"video-covered) timepoints of all kept trials; class 2 marks \"\n   875→                              \"timepoints where the feature is not tracked or the video does \"\n   876→                              \"not cover the time bin.\",\n   877→            \"reference\": \"Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nature Neuroscience 2025, \"\n   878→                         \"doi:10.1038/s41593-024-01859-1; data doi:10.5281/zenodo.13941415\",\n   879→            \"session_info\": [r[\"info\"] for _, r in kept],\n   880→            \"sessions_dropped\": dropped,\n   881→        },\n   882→    }\n   883→\n   884→    # ---- summary ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n   885→    ntr = [len(n) for n in data[\"neural\"]]\n   886→    nun = [n[0].shape[0] for n in data[\"neural\"]]\n   887→    print(\"\\n=== converted dataset ===\")\n   888→    print(f\"sessions           : {len(ntr)}\")\n   889→    print(f\"subjects           : {len(subjects)} {subjects}\")\n   890→    print(f\"trials             : {sum(ntr)} (min {min(ntr)}, max {max(ntr)}, \"\n   891→          f\"mean {np.mean(ntr):.1f})\")\n   892→    print(f\"units              : {sum(nun)} (min n//2 taps, which\n    is evaluated directly (exact, no FFT round-off).\n    \"\"\"\n    n = kern.size\n    half = (n - 1) // 2\n    if bctype == \"reflect\":\n        xp = np.concatenate([x[:n], x], == 0.0:\n            continue\n        j = k - half                      # delay in samples (>= 0)\n        if j == 0:\n            out += w * xp\n        else:\n            out[j:] += w * xp[:-j]\n --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef select_clusters(obj, probes):\n    \"\"\"findClusters(quality, {'all'}): keep everything except garbage / \"\"\"Boolean mask of trials for which spikes were actually recorded.\n\n    In a few sessions the SpikeGLX recording stops before the behavioural session does\n    (JEB24 2023-10-23 and 2023-11-03 Neural\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef bin_and_smooth(obj, cluster_ids, align_times, ntrials):\n    \"\"\"getSeq.m -- (ntrials, --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Video-derived outputs\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef interp_trace(src_t, src_y, taxis):\n    \"\"\"interp1(src_t, src_y, taxis) -- linear, NaN outside the source range.\"\"\"\n    return np.interp(taxis, src_t, src_y, left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n\n\ndef interp_feature(ts, featix, ft, align_time, taxis):\n    \"\"\"findPosition.m -- interpolate one DLC contiguous visible run (np.gradient,\n    i.e. MATLAB's gradient()), so a gap never contaminates a neighbouring sample.\n    Returns NaN where the feature is not visible.\n    \"\"\"\n    visible = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)\n    vx = np.full(x.shape, np.nan)\n    vy = np.full(y.shape, np.nan)\n    idx = np.flatnonzero(visible)\n    if idx.size == 0:\n        return np.full(x.shape, np.nan)\n    brk = np.flatnonzero(np.diff(idx) > 1)\n    starts = np.concatenate(([0], brk + 1))\n    stops = np.concatenate((brk, [idx.size - 1]))\n    for s, e in zip(starts, stops):\n        seg = idx[s:e + 1]\n        if seg.size == 1:\n            vx[seg] = 0.0\n            vy[seg] = 0.0\n        else:\n            vx[seg] = np.gradient(x[seg])\n            vy[seg] = np.gradient(y[seg])\n    if subtract_baseline:\n        # findVelocity.m: basederiv = median(diff(pos),'omitnan'); xvel/yvel -= basederiv(1)\n        d = np.diff(np.stack([x, y], axis=1), axis=0)\n        with     if names is None:\n            continue\n        names = [str(s) for s in np.ravel(np.asarray(names, dtype=object))]\n        if name in names:\n            return names.index(name)\n    raise ValueError(f\"DLC feature {name!r} not found in view {view + 1}\")\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Per-session conversion\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef process_session(args):\n    anm, date, probes, task = args\n    t0 = time.time()\n    obj = load_obj(anm, np.full(ntrials_all, 2, dtype=np.int8)                     # 2 = none\n    lick[(L & hit) | (R & miss)] = 0                                  # left\n    lick[(R & hit) | (L & miss)] = 1                                  # right\n    lick[no] = 2                                                      # getPrevChoice: NaN on ignore\n    context = np.where(autowater, 0, 1).astype(np.int8)               # 0 = WC, 1 = DR\n    outcome = np.full(ntrials_all, 0, dtype=np.int8)                  # 0 = incorrect\n    outcome[hit] = 1                                                  # 1 = correct\n       else:\n            thr = np.nan\n        return out, thr\n\n    tongue_c, thr_tongue = discretise(tongue_v)\n    paw_c, thr_paw = discretise(paw_v)\n    me_c, thr_me = discretise(me_v)\n\n    # Commented-out entries are sessions the authors excluded. |\n    39→| `loadSessionData` | `DataLoadingScripts/loadSessionData.m` | LOADING | Loops sessions × probes, calls `processData`, concatenates 2-probe sessions |\n    40→| `processData` | `DataLoadingScripts/processData.m` | PROCESSING | Orchestrates `findTrials` → `findClusters` → `alignSpikes` → `getSeq` → `removeLowFRClusters` → `baselineFR` |\n    41→| `findTrials` | `DataLoadingScripts/findTrials.m` | CURATION | Evaluates `params.condition` strings (e.g. `'R&hit&~stim.enable&~autowater&~early'`) against `obj.bp` fields to get trial indices per condition |\n    42→| `findClusters` | `DataLoadingScripts/findClusters.m` | CURATION | With `params.quality={'all'}` keeps every cluster whose (trimmed) quality label is **not** `garbage`, `gabrga`, `noisy`, `real?` (case-sensitive) |\n    43→| `alignSpikes` | `DataLoadingScripts/alignSpikes.m` | ALIGNMENT | `clu.trialtm_aligned = clu.trialtm - ev.(alignEvent)(clu.trial)`; for us `alignEvent='goCue'` |\n    44→| `getSeq` | `DataLoadingScripts/getSeq.m` | PROCESSING | `edges = tmin:dt:tmax`; `obj.time = edges(1:end-1)+dt/2`; per trial `histc` spike counts → `/dt` → `mySmooth(...,params.smooth,params.bctype)` → `obj.trialdat` (time × units × trials), units = spikes/s |\n    45→| `removeLowFRClusters` | `DataLoadingScripts/removeLowFRClusters.m` | CURATION | Drops units with mean PSTH firing rate ≤ `params.lowFR` (1 Hz in all figure scripts) |\n    46→| `mySmooth` | `utils/mySmooth.m` | PROCESSING | Causal Gaussian filter: `gausswin(15)`, first `floor(15/2)=7` taps zeroed, normalised, `conv(...,'same')`; `'reflect'` boundary = prepend first 15 samples, then trim |\n    47→| `findVideoOffset` | `funcs/findVideoOffset.m` | ALIGNMENT | `vidshift = mode(sglx.bitcode.bitstart)/sglx.fs − mode(bp.ev.bitStart)` (≈0.49 s, 0.99 s for JEB19). Video time in trial coordinates = `frameTimes − vidshift` |\n    48→| `loadMotionEnergy` | `DataLoadingScripts/loadMotionEnergy.m` | LOADING / ALIGNMENT | Loads `motionEnergy_<ANM>_<DATE>.mat`, then `interp1(frameTimes − vidshift − alignTime, me, obj.time)` and `fillmissing('nearest')`; `me.move = me.data > me.moveThresh` |\n    49→| `findPosition` | `funcs/kinematics/findPosition.m` | PROCESSING / ALIGNMENT | Per DLC feature: take `ts(:,1:2,featix)` (x,y), interp onto `obj.time` using `frameTimes − vidshift − alignTime`; **tongue is not smoothed and NaNs are kept**; all other features `fillmissing('nearest')` |\n    50→| `findVelocity` | `funcs/kinematics/findVelocity.m` | PROCESSING | `xvel = gradient(x)`, `yvel = gradient(y)`; non-tongue features get a `median(diff(pos))` baseline-drift subtraction and `fillmissing('nearest')`; **tongue NaN velocities are set to 0** |\n    51→| `getKinematics` / `getKinematicsFromVideo` | `funcs/kinematics/*.m` | PROCESSING | Assembles (xdisp, ydisp, xvel, yvel) per feature per view + tongue angle/length + motion energy into `kin.dat` (time × trials × features) |\n    52→| `getPrevChoice` | `funcs/getPrevChoice.m` | OUTPUT DEF | **Definition of choice/lick direction**: `choice = (R & hit) | (L & miss)` (1 = right), `NaN` on `no` (ignore) trials |\n    53→| `getOutcome` | `funcs/getOutcome.m` | OUTPUT DEF | `outcome = bp.hit`, `NaN` on `no` (ignore) trials |\n    54→| `UseInclusionCritera` / `RemoveUnwantedSessions` | `utils/*.m` | CURATION | Drop sessions with ≤40 right-correct or ≤40 left-correct DR trials (used for a subset of figures) |\n    55→| `NeuralChoiceDecoding.m` / `NeuralContextDecoding.m` | `ChoiceContextDecoding/` | ANALYSIS | The paper's own decoders: input `obj.trialdat` (time × units × trials), min-max normalised to [-1,1], 75 ms bins, logistic regression per time bin |\n    56→\n    57→### Parameters used consistently across every figure script\n    58→```\n    59→params.alignEvent = 'goCue';\n    60→params.lowFR      = 1;            % Hz\n    61→params.tmin       = -2.5;         % s relative to align event\n    62→params.tmax       =  2.5;         % s\n    63→params.dt         = 1/100;        % 10 ms bins\n    64→params.smooth     = 15;           % causal gaussian window (samples)\n    65→params.bctype     = 'reflect';\n    66→params.quality    = {'all'};      % keep all but garbage/noisy\n    67→params.advance_movement = 0;      % no neural/video lag\n    68→```\n    69→(`getDefaultParams.m` has `lowFR=0.5`, `dt=1/200`, but **every** actual analysis script —\n    70→`Scripts/Figure*`, `Scripts/EDFigure*`, `Behavior/*`, `WorkingWithDataObjs.m` — overrides these\n    71→with the values above. The above is therefore the reference processing.)\n    72→\n    73→### Notes\n    74→- Electrophysiology: spikes are already sorted; units must be **quality-filtered**\n    75→  (`findClusters`) and **firing-rate filtered** (`removeLowFRClusters`, >1 Hz). No dF/F\n    76→  (this is ephys, not imaging).\n    77→- The neural data the paper feeds to its own decoders is `obj.trialdat`: **smoothed,\n    78→  binned single-trial firing rates in spikes/s**, go-cue aligned, 10 ms bins, [-2.5, 2.5] s.\n    79→- Video is sampled at 400 Hz and must be shifted by `findVideoOffset` before being\n    80→  aligned to the go cue; there is no additional neural/video lag (`advance_movement = 0`).\n    81→\n    82→---\n    83→\n    84→## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n    85→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    86→\n    87→### Data Structure\n    88→`/app/data` has four subdirectories:\n    89→\n    90→| Directory | Contents | Neural data? 183→JEB6 2021-04-18, JEB7 2021-04-29/30, EKH1 2021-08-07, EKH3 2021-08-11,\n   184→JGR2 2021-11-16/17, JGR3 2021-11-18, JEB19 2023-04-18/19/20/21.\n   185→Their quality-filtered unit count is **528** and the unit-curation rule.\n   188→\n   189→### Processing Details\n   190→- **Temporal alignment**: go cue (`obj.bp.ev.goCue`), which in WC (autowater) trials is the\n   191→  time the water drop is presented. Window [-2.5, +2.5] s.\n   192→- **Temporal binning**: 10 ms bins; spike counts → rate (÷dt) → causal Gaussian smoothing\n   193→  (`mySmooth`, window 15 samples = 150 ms, 'reflect' boundary).\n   194→- **Video alignment**: `frameTimes − vidshift − goCue`, `vidshift` from `findVideoOffset`\n   195→  (0.49 s for all sessions except JEB19 where it is 0.99 s). Linear interpolation onto the\n   196→  10 ms neural time base. No neural/video lag.\n   197→\n   198→### Curation Steps\n   199→\n   200→**Neuron curation rules**:\n   201→1. Only the ALM probe(s) designated in the reference `load<ANM>_ALMVideo.m` scripts.\n   202→2. Drop clusters whose trimmed quality label is `garbage`, `gabrga`, `noisy` or `real?`\n   203→   (`findClusters` with `quality={'all'}`; note the comparison is case-sensitive, so the single\n   204→   `Noisy` label in JEB6 is kept — reproduced exactly).\n   205→3. Drop clusters with mean firing rate ≤ 1 Hz over the analysis window (`lowFR = 1`).\n   206→\n   207→**Trial curation rules**:\n   208→1. Drop `early` (early-lick) trials — explicitly excluded from all analyses in the Methods and\n   209→   by `~early` in every `params.condition`.\n   210→2. Drop photoinactivation trials (`stim.enable == 1`) — `~stim.enable` in every\n   211→   `params.condition`; these perturb both neural activity and behaviour.\n   212→3. **Keep** `hit`, `miss` and `no` (ignore) trials, and keep both `autowater` (WC) and\n   213→   non-autowater (DR) trials: the decoder task requires `ignore` as an outcome class,\n   214→   `none` as a lick-direction class and `WC`/`DR` as context classes.\n   215→\n   216→**Session curation rule**: ≥10 units after neuron curation (Methods).\n   217→\n   218→### Decoders Trained (in the paper)\n   219→| Decoded variable | Method | Accuracy |\n   220→|---|---|---|\n   221→| Choice (L vs R), from CD_choice projection | logistic regression, ROC | AUC 0.86 ± 0.11 (ED Fig. 2c) |\n   222→| Choice (L vs R), from neural population | logistic regression per 75 ms bin, 4-fold CV | ≈0.5 (chance) before sample, rising through the delay to ≈0.9 after the go cue (Fig. 3b) |\n   223→| Choice, from video kinematics | same | rises from chance after the sample tone (Fig. 3b) |\n   224→| Context (DR vs WC), from neural population | same | well above chance (≈0.7) even during the ITI, ≈0.9 around the response epoch (Fig. 4b) |\n   225→| Context, from video kinematics | same | similar timecourse to neural (Fig. 4b) |\n   226→| CD_context from kinematics (regression) | ridge regression | R² = 0.49 ± 0.22 (n = 12 sessions) |\n   227→\n   228→These are the quantitative expectations against which the converted-data decoder is compared\n   229→in Steps 11–12. Note the paper's decoders are trained **per session** on **balanced** subsets\n   230→of correct left/right trials, whereas the provided harness trains one shared decoder over all\n   231→sessions on all trials; exact numbers are therefore not directly comparable, but the\n   232→qualitative expectation (choice and context well above chance, highest after the go cue) is.\n   233→\n   234→---\n   235→\n   236→## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n   237→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   238→\n   239→### Discrepancies Found\n   240→| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Paper says | Resolution |\n   241→|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n   242→| Analysis parameters | `getDefaultParams.m`: `dt=1/200`, `lowFR=0.5` vs every figure script: `dt=1/100`, `lowFR=1` | — | 1 Hz FR threshold; 10 ms consistent with Fig. 2/3 panels | Use the figure-script values (`dt=1/100`, `lowFR=1`); `getDefaultParams` is unused by the shipped analyses |\n   243→| Bin width comment | `WorkingWithDataObjs.m` comment says \"use a 5 ms bin width\" but the code sets `params.dt = 1/100` | — | — | Comment is stale; use 10 ms |\n   244→| # mice, fixed delay | 10 distinct animals in the load scripts | 10 animals over 25 files | \"25 sessions, nine mice\" | Paper appears to under-count by one (EKH1 is absent from `NullPotent/SessionMeta.csv`, which lists 24 of the 25 sessions and 9 mice). Follow the reference **load scripts** for probe selection and label every included unit `ALM`, as the paper does. |\n   248→| Context proxy | `params.condition` uses `autowater` to separate WC from DR; tutorial: \"this field can be used as a proxy for obtaining water-cued blocks and delayed-response blocks\" | `autowater` forms contiguous blocks starting ≈trial 100 in exactly 12 sessions | \"A behavioral session began with approximately 100 DR trials and was then followed by alternating blocks of WC and DR trials\" | Use `autowater` as the context label.
